```
# Phase 68 Learnings: web-integration-rotation-ux-and-durable-client-state

## Decisions

### Hoist anti-rollback logic to the SDK tier; web owns only a thin adapter

The durable high-water state machine and fail-closed `enforceResolved` gate live in `packages/sdk` (Vitest-tested), not in a web module; the concrete IndexedDB `HighWaterStore` is a thin, untested adapter deferred to 68-06.

**Rationale:** Per docs/TESTING.md, apps/web is NOT unit-tested — logic must live where it can be unit-proven, with the web layer covered only by web-e2e.
**Source:** 68-01-PLAN.md

---

### Doctrine correction: no fake-indexeddb shim, zero apps/web unit tests

The initial plan set's IndexedDB test-env shim was explicitly removed. Durable-store behavior is proven by real IndexedDB in web-e2e (a "persists across reload" claim MUST be a real browser reload — in-memory-map unit tests are rejected as "in-memory only"), and the pure logic is proven in the SDK over an injected map. SC#5 gates that `find apps/web/src -name "*.spec.ts"` stays empty.
30
```

```
253	  /**
254	   * Persist a rotated readDescriptorRef and rootGeneration on an existing share.
255	   * Only the sharer (owner) can update it — the owner drives grant re-mint on
256	   * key rotation (D-10/D-11, 68-07 owner reconcile). The server never
257	   * re-encrypts — it persists the client-supplied ciphertext as-is.
258	   */
259	  async updateGrant(
260	    shareId: string,
261	    sharerId: string,
262	    readDescriptorRef: string,
263	    rootGeneration: string
264	  ): Promise<void> {
265	    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
266	
267	    if (!share) {
268	      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
269	    }
270	
271	    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
272	      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update the grant');
273	    }
274	
275	    // Anti-rollback: the grant's rootGeneration seeds the recipient's durable
276	    // generation floor on first contact, so a replayed/duplicate PATCH must
277	    // never regress it below the stored value.
278	    if (BigInt(rootGeneration) < BigInt(share.rootGeneration)) {
279	      throw new ConflictException(
280	        'rootGeneration regression rejected: grants only advance the generation'
281	      );
282	    }
283	
284	    share.readDescriptorRef = Buffer.from(readDescriptorRef, 'hex');
285	    share.rootGeneration = rootGeneration;
286	    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
287	  }
288	}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/shares.service.ts",
  "old_string": "  async updateGrant(\n    shareId: string,\n    sharerId: string,\n    readDescriptorRef: string,\n    rootGeneration: string\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });\n\n    if (!share) {\n      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');\n    }\n\n    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {\n      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update the grant');\n    }\n\n    // Anti-rollback: the grant's rootGeneration seeds the recipient's durable\n    // generation floor on first contact, so a replayed/duplicate PATCH must\n    // never regress it below the stored value.\n    if (BigInt(rootGeneration) < BigInt(share.rootGeneration)) {\n      throw new ConflictException(\n        'rootGeneration regression rejected: grants only advance the generation'\n      );\n    }\n\n    share.readDescriptorRef = Buffer.from(readDescriptorRef, 'hex');\n    share.rootGeneration = rootGeneration;\n    await this.shareRepo.save(share);\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "  async updateGrant(\n    shareId: string,\n    sharerId: string,\n    readDescriptorRef: string,\n    rootGeneration: string\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    // Row-locked transaction: the anti-rollback check below must see the row\n    // a concurrent PATCH just committed, not a stale pre-race read (TOCTOU).\n    await this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {\n      const share = await manager.findOne(Share, {\n        where: { id: shareId },\n        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },\n      });\n\n      if (!share) {\n        throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');\n      }\n\n      if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {\n        throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update the grant');\n      }\n\n      // Anti-rollback: the grant's rootGeneration seeds the recipient's durable\n      // generation floor on first contact, so a replayed/duplicate PATCH must\n      // never regress it below the stored value.\n      if (BigInt(rootGeneration) < BigInt(share.rootGeneration)) {\n        throw new ConflictException(\n          'rootGeneration regression rejected: grants only advance the generation'\n        );\n      }\n\n      share.readDescriptorRef = Buffer.from(readDescriptorRef, 'hex');\n      share.rootGeneration = rootGeneration;\n      await manager.save(share);\n    });\n  }\n}"
}
```

```
=== [0] apps/web/src/services/rotation-driver.service.ts:80 (id: PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM6NwKCe)
<a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=9" align="top"></a> **IDB connections opened per-operation and never closed**

Both `openJobDB()` here and `openRotationDB()` in `rotation-state.service.ts` open a fresh `IDBDatabase` connection on every `putJobCheckpoint`, `deleteJobCheckpoint`, `getAllJobCheckpoints`, `idbGet`, and `idbPut` call, and no connection is ever closed (`db.close()`). Each open-but-not-closed handle registers as a connection holder. While this is harmless today (DB_VERSION is fixed at 1), any future bump to `JOB_DB_VERSION` or `DB_VERSION` will send a `versionchange` event to all accumulated open handles; since no `onversionchange` handler is registered on these connections, the upgrade will block indefinitely until the page is closed. The same pattern appears in `rotation-state.service.ts`. A cached singleton conne

=== [1] apps/web/src/services/owner-reconcile.service.ts:210 (id: PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM6NwKDF)
<a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=9" align="top"></a> **`GET /shares/sent` fired on every `folder:updated` event without a local short-circuit**

`runOwnerReconcileForFolder` is invoked from `useAuth.ts` on every `folder:updated` SDK event (rename, move, delete, and any upload that updates folder metadata). Each call issues a fresh `sharesControllerGetSentShares()` API request before checking whether the updated folder is even a grant root. For users with no sent shares, or whose updated folder is not a grant root, this generates a redundant network round-trip on every mutation. The in-memory `sentShares` from `useShareStore` is already available and up-to-date — a quick check against it before making the API call (mirroring `getActiveGrantRootIpnsNames` in `rotation-driver.service.ts`, which uses the store directly) would shor

=== [2] apps/web/src/hooks/useMutationFailureUx.ts:96 (id: PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM6NwKDc)
<a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=9" align="top"></a> **Manual retry from exhaustion toast can produce cascading stacked toasts**

`dispatchDeferExhausted` attaches an `onClick` that calls `retryAfterExhaustion(mutationFn)`, which internally calls `runWithFailureUx(mutationFn)` with a fresh 5-attempt budget. If that second run also exhausts all `ReconcileStaleError` retries, `dispatchDeferExhausted(mutationFn)` is called again — adding a new "Couldn't complete securely — retry." toast on top of the first (the old toast is not dismissed). On a persistent reconcile failure, every user click on "Retry" produces another stacked toast. Consider dismissing or replacing the originating notification before re-running, or removing the action on the second dispatch.

=== [3] apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts:43 (id: PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM6NwKD7)
<a href="#"><img alt="P2" src="https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p2.svg?v=9" align="top"></a> **Stub wrapped in `runWithFailureUx` fires `maybeWarnDegradedCache` spuriously**

`handleUpdateFile` is not yet implemented; it always throws before touching the rotation cache. Wrapping it in `runWithFailureUx` means the `finally` block calls `maybeWarnDegradedCache()` on every invocation. If the rotation-state store has degraded this session, the user sees "Secure cache unavailable — falling back to server verification" as a result of clicking "update file" — an operation that never reached the rotation path at all. The wrapper comment says the eventual SDK call should be inside `runWithFailureUx`, which is correct; but the current stub should just throw directly so the degraded-cache notice is not triggered by unimplemented stubs.
```

```
import { create } from 'zustand';

export type Notification = {
  id: string;
  type: 'info' | 'warning' | 'error';
  message: string;
  createdAt: number;
  action?: { label: string; onClick: () => void };
};

type NotificationState = {
  notifications: Notification[];
  addNotification: (
    type: Notification['type'],
    message: string,
    action?: Notification['action']
  ) => void;
  dismissNotification: (id: string) => void;
  clearNotifications: () => void;
};

export const useNotificationStore = create<NotificationState>((set) => ({
  notifications: [],

  addNotification: (type, message, action) =>
    set((state) => ({
      notifications: [
        ...state.notifications,
        {
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          type,
          message,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          action,
        },
      ],
    })),

  dismissNotification: (id) =>
    set((state) => ({
      notifications: state.notifications.filter((n) => n.id !== id),
    })),

  clearNotifications: () => set({ notifications: [] }),
}));
```

**`MAX_NODES` cutoff leaves un-dequeued readKeys unzeroed.**

**`rotationResult.readKey` is never zeroed after the defensive copy — key material left live in memory.**

`oldFolderKey.fill(0)` zeroes the previous key, but the freshly-minted `rotationResult.readKey` (the buffer returned by `rotateReadFromNode`, per its own `@security` doc, whose "caller becomes the terminal owner") is copied into `folderTree` via `new Uint8Array(...)` and then simply left referenced only by the local `rotationResult` variable — it's never `.fill(0)`'d. This is inconsistent with the rigorous D-09 zeroing pattern used everywhere else in this file (e.g. `childReadKey.fill(0)`, `item.nodeReadKey.fill(0)` in engine.ts).

**`MAX_NODES` cutoff leaves un-dequeued readKeys unzeroed.**

When the walk hits `MAX_NODES` with items still in `queue`, the loop exits without ever dequeuing (and thus never `finally`-zeroing) those remaining entries' `readKey` buffers. This is precisely the "pathological/corrupted tree" scenario the guard is meant to protect against, so it's the one case where key hygiene is most likely to matter.

<details>
<summary>🔒 Proposed fix</summary>

```diff

       ...
     }
+    // Zero any readKeys left un-dequeued when the MAX_NODES bound was hit.
+    for (const remaining of queue) {
+      if (!remaining.isRoot) remaining.readKey.fill(0);
+    }
 
     return { readableIpnsNames, unreadableIpnsNames };
```
</details>

<summary>📝 Committable suggestion</summary>

> Carefully review the code before committing. Ensure that it accurately replaces the highlighted code, contains no missing lines, and has no issues with indentation. Thoroughly test & benchmark the code to ensure it meets the requirements.

```suggestion
  private async enumerateMoveDescendants(
    rootIpnsName: string,
    rootReadKey: Uint8Array,
    rootKind: PublishedNode['kind']
  ): Promise<{ readableIpnsNames: string[]; unreadableIpnsNames: string[] }> {
    const readableIpnsNames: string[] = [];
    const unreadableIpnsNames: string[] = [];

    if (rootKind !== 'folder' && rootKind !== 'root') {
      return { readableIpnsNames, unreadableIpnsNames };
    }

    const visited = new Set<string>([rootIpnsName]);
    const queue: Array<{ ipnsName: string; readKey: Uint8Array; isRoot: boolean }> = [
      { ipnsName: rootIpnsName, readKey: rootReadKey, isRoot: true },
    ];
    // Bound the walk so a pathological/corrupted tree can't hang the move.
    const MAX_NODES = 2000;
    let processed = 0;

    while (queue.length > 0 && processed < MAX_NODES) {
      const current = queue.shift();
      if (!current) break;
      processed++;

      try {
        let node: CoreNode;
        try {
          const record = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(current.ipnsName, this.ctx);
          if (!record) continue;
          const raw = await sdkCore.fetchFromIpfs(this.ctx, record.cid);
          const published = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(raw)) as PublishedNode;
          node = await unsealNode(published, current.readKey);
        } catch {
          continue;
        }

        for (const child of node.children ?? []) {
          if (visited.has(child.ipnsName)) continue;
          visited.add(child.ipnsName);

          try {
            const childRecord = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(child.ipnsName, this.ctx);
            if (!childRecord) throw new Error('child IPNS record not found');
            const rawChild = await sdkCore.fetchFromIpfs(this.ctx, childRecord.cid);
            const childPublished = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(rawChild)) as PublishedNode;
            const childReadKey = await unsealChildReadKey(
              child.readKeySealed,
              current.readKey,
              childPublished.id,
              childPublished.kind,
              child.generation
            );
            readableIpnsNames.push(child.ipnsName);
            queue.push({ ipnsName: child.ipnsName, readKey: childReadKey, isRoot: false });
          } catch {
            unreadableIpnsNames.push(child.ipnsName);
          }
        }
      } finally {
        // The children loop unseals with this key, so it may only be zeroed
        // after the loop (this queue entry is the key's terminal owner).
        if (!current.isRoot) current.readKey.fill(0);
      }
    }
    // Zero any readKeys left un-dequeued when the MAX_NODES bound was hit.
    for (const remaining of queue) {
      if (!remaining.isRoot) remaining.readKey.fill(0);
    }

    return { readableIpnsNames, unreadableIpnsNames };
  }
```

</details>

<!-- suggestion_end -->

**`enforceResolved` doesn't validate its own inputs, only stored values — NaN/invalid params silently bypass the regression check.**

`isValidFloorValue`/`readFloor` only guard values read back from the store. The live `generation`/`seq` params compared directly here (`generation < generationFloor`, `seq < seqFloor`) are never validated. Since `NaN < x` and `x < NaN` are always `false` in JS, a `NaN` (or otherwise malformed) `generation`/`seq` silently passes the "regression" check instead of being rejected — defeating the fail-closed guarantee this module documents as its purpose.

```
32	/**
33	 * Fetch + decode every share the owner has sent.
34	 *
35	 * `recipientPublicKey` is hex-encoded on the wire (0x04...); decoded to
36	 * `Uint8Array` here for the ECIES re-wrap inside the SDK driver.
37	 *
38	 * `isRevoked` is always `false`: this project's shares table uses hard-delete
39	 * revocation (D-11 forward-only revocation, project convention -- revoke =
40	 * DELETE, never a soft `revoked_at` flag), so a revoked grant row is removed
41	 * from the table entirely and never appears in `GET /shares/sent`. Every row
42	 * returned here is therefore an active (non-revoked) grant.
43	 */
44	async function decodeSentGrants(): Promise<DecodedSentGrant[]> {
45	  const { shares } = await sharesControllerGetSentShares();
46	  const decoded: DecodedSentGrant[] = [];
47	  for (const share of shares) {
48	    try {
49	      // Wire format is bare hex, but normalize a 0x prefix defensively --
50	      // hexToBytes would otherwise choke and one bad row must not abort the
51	      // whole reconcile sweep.
52	      const bareHex = share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')
53	        ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)
54	        : share.recipientPublicKey;

56	        shareId: share.shareId,

60	        rootIpnsName: share.rootIpnsName,
61	      });
62	    } catch (error) {
63	      logger.error(
64	        `[owner-reconcile] Skipping sent grant ${share.shareId}: recipientPublicKey decode failed:`,
65	        safeError(error)
66	      );
67	    }
68	  }
69	  return decoded;
70	}

110	}

118	}

121	  return {

129	/**
130	 * Eager owner-reconcile sweep (D-11): run once per login/app-open.
131	 *
132	 * For every distinct `rootNodeId` among the owner's sent grants, look up the
133	 * node's CURRENT `folderKey`/`nodeGeneration` from the SDK's in-memory
134	 * `FolderTree` (populated as folders are loaded) and drive the SDK
135	 * owner-reconcile driver so any grant left dangling by a write-recipient's
136	 * independent unlink+bin (while this session was offline) gets re-minted or
137	 * deleted.
138	 *
139	 * Best-effort: a root whose folder is not currently loaded in the
140	 * `FolderTree` is skipped -- there is no in-memory readKey to re-mint with
141	 * for it yet. The opportunistic post-mutation trigger (68-08) covers the
142	 * live case where a fresh readKey is available immediately after the
143	 * owner's own rotation. Fire-and-forget; errors are captured and logged,
144	 * never thrown to the caller (must not block the login return path).
145	 */
146	export async function triggerOwnerReconcileOnLogin(): Promise<void> {
147	  if (!hasSdkClient()) return;
148	
149	  let decoded: DecodedSentGrant[];
150	  try {
151	    decoded = await decodeSentGrants();
152	  } catch (error) {
153	    logger.error(

155	      safeError(error)
156	    );
157	    return;
158	  }
159	
160	  // The client can be torn down (logout) while the fetch above was in flight.
161	  if (!hasSdkClient()) return;
162	
163	  const distinctRoots = new Map<string, string>(); // rootNodeId -> rootIpnsName
164	  for (const grant of decoded) {
165	    if (!distinctRoots.has(grant.rootNodeId)) {
166	      distinctRoots.set(grant.rootNodeId, grant.rootIpnsName);
167	    }
168	  }
169	
170	  const client = getSdkClient();
171	  const folderTree = client.getFolderTree();
172	
173	  for (const [rootNodeId, rootIpnsName] of distinctRoots) {

198	 * Opportunistic post-mutation owner-reconcile trigger (D-11), scoped to a
199	 * SINGLE root -- the folder the client just mutated -- rather than the full
200	 * login sweep above. Wired from `useAuth.ts` after the client's

202	 * this folder happens immediately after the owner's own scope-exit
203	 * rotation, without waiting for the next login sweep.
204	 *
205	 * Sourced the same way as the eager login sweep: the CURRENT
206	 * `folderKey`/`nodeGeneration` from the SDK's in-memory `FolderTree`.

209	 * errors are captured and logged, never thrown to the caller.
210	 */
211	export async function runOwnerReconcileForFolder(rootIpnsName: string): Promise<void> {
212	  if (!hasSdkClient()) return;
213	
214	  let decoded: DecodedSentGrant[];
215	  try {
216	    decoded = await decodeSentGrants();
217	  } catch (error) {
218	    logger.error(

220	      safeError(error)
221	    );
222	    return;
223	  }
224	
225	  // The client can be torn down (logout) while the fetch above was in flight.
226	  if (!hasSdkClient()) return;
227	
228	  const grant = decoded.find((candidate) => candidate.rootIpnsName === rootIpnsName);
229	  if (!grant) return; // not a grant root -- nothing to reconcile
230	
231	  const client = getSdkClient();
232	  const folderState = client.getFolderTree().get(rootIpnsName);
233	  if (!folderState) return; // best-effort skip, same as the eager login sweep
234	
235	  try {
236	    await runOwnerReconcile(
237	      grant.rootNodeId,
238	      folderState.folderKey,
239	      folderState.nodeGeneration,
240	      makeReconcileJob(grant.rootNodeId),
241	      buildReconcileCtx(),
242	      webOwnerReconcileTransport
243	    );
244	  } catch (error) {
245	    logger.error(
246	      `[owner-reconcile] Opportunistic reconcile failed for root ${grant.rootNodeId}:`,
247	      safeError(error)
248	    );
249	  }
250	}
```

```
3	 *

23	/** A durable key-value seam for a single high-water floor (generation or seq). */
24	export interface HighWaterStore {
25	  get(nodeId: string): Promise<number | undefined>;
26	  put(nodeId: string, value: number): Promise<void>;
27	}

38	/** Thrown when a resolved generation regresses below the durable generation floor. */
39	export class GenerationRegressionError extends Error {
40	  constructor(nodeId: string, floor: number, attempted: number) {
41	    super(
42	      `Generation regression rejected for node ${nodeId}: floor=${floor}, attempted=${attempted}`
43	    );
44	    this.name = 'GenerationRegressionError';
45	  }
46	}
47	
48	/** Thrown when a resolved seq regresses below the durable seq floor (or cold-device versionFloor). */
49	export class SequenceRegressionError extends Error {
50	  constructor(nodeId: string, floor: number, attempted: number) {
51	    super(
52	      `Sequence regression rejected for node ${nodeId}: floor=${floor}, attempted=${attempted}`
53	    );
54	    this.name = 'SequenceRegressionError';
55	  }
56	}
57	
58	/**
59	 * Validates a value read back from a HighWaterStore. Fail-closed (V5):
60	 * anything that is not a non-negative safe integer is treated as absent
61	 * rather than coerced to a low floor.
62	 */
63	function isValidFloorValue(value: unknown): value is number {
64	  return (
65	    typeof value === 'number' &&
66	    Number.isInteger(value) &&
67	    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
68	    value >= 0
69	  );
70	}
71	
72	/**
73	 * Reads a floor from the store, applying V5 fail-closed validation.
74	 * A malformed stored value (negative, fractional, NaN, non-numeric) is
75	 * treated as absent -- never coerced to a low floor.
76	 */
77	async function readFloor(store: HighWaterStore, nodeId: string): Promise<number | undefined> {
78	  const raw = await store.get(nodeId);
79	  return isValidFloorValue(raw) ? raw : undefined;
80	}
81	
82	/**
83	 * Conditionally raises a floor to `candidate` only if it is higher than the
84	 * current stored value (monotonic-max). Returns the resulting floor.
85	 */
86	async function bumpFloor(
87	  store: HighWaterStore,
88	  nodeId: string,
89	  candidate: number
90	): Promise<number> {
91	  const current = await readFloor(store, nodeId);
92	  if (current === undefined || candidate > current) {
93	    await store.put(nodeId, candidate);
94	    return candidate;
95	  }
96	  return current;
97	}

99	/** The durable rotation high-water state machine returned by createRotationHighWater. */
100	export interface RotationHighWater {
101	  getGenerationFloor(nodeId: string): Promise<number | undefined>;
102	  bumpGeneration(nodeId: string, generation: number): Promise<void>;
103	  /** Owner-vouched first-contact seed -- raises the generation floor only if higher (never lowers it). */
104	  seedFromGrant(nodeId: string, rootGeneration: number): Promise<void>;
105	  getSeqFloor(nodeId: string): Promise<number | undefined>;
106	  bumpSeq(nodeId: string, seq: number): Promise<void>;
107	  /**
108	   * Fail-closed pre-unseal gate: compares a freshly-resolved seq/generation
109	   * against the stored floors. Throws a distinguishable regression error on
110	   * any regression; on first contact (no local seq floor) applies the
111	   * cold-device versionFloor gate; otherwise bumps both floors monotonic-max.
112	   *
113	   * This is a pure pass/throw decision -- it never returns or computes any
114	   * AAD/unseal parameter.
115	   */
116	  enforceResolved(params: EnforceResolvedParams): Promise<void>;
117	}
118	
119	/**
120	 * Creates a durable rotation high-water state machine over two injected
121	 * HighWaterStore seams -- one for the generation floor, one for the seq
122	 * floor. Holds no in-instance cache: every read/write goes through the
123	 * injected stores.
124	 */
125	export function createRotationHighWater(
126	  generationStore: HighWaterStore,
127	  seqStore: HighWaterStore
128	): RotationHighWater {
129	  return {
130	    async getGenerationFloor(nodeId) {
131	      return readFloor(generationStore, nodeId);
132	    },
133	
134	    async bumpGeneration(nodeId, generation) {
135	      await bumpFloor(generationStore, nodeId, generation);
136	    },
137	
138	    async seedFromGrant(nodeId, rootGeneration) {
139	      await bumpFloor(generationStore, nodeId, rootGeneration);
140	    },
141	
142	    async getSeqFloor(nodeId) {
143	      return readFloor(seqStore, nodeId);
144	    },
145	
146	    async bumpSeq(nodeId, seq) {
147	      await bumpFloor(seqStore, nodeId, seq);
148	    },
149	
150	    async enforceResolved({ nodeId, seq, generation, versionFloor }) {
151	      const generationFloor = await readFloor(generationStore, nodeId);
152	      if (generationFloor !== undefined && generation < generationFloor) {
153	        throw new GenerationRegressionError(nodeId, generationFloor, generation);
154	      }
155	
156	      const seqFloor = await readFloor(seqStore, nodeId);
157	      if (seqFloor === undefined) {
158	        // Cold-device / first-contact: apply the owner-vouched versionFloor gate.
159	        if (seq < versionFloor) {
160	          throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, versionFloor, seq);
161	        }
162	      } else if (seq < seqFloor) {
163	        throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, seqFloor, seq);
164	      }
165	
166	      await bumpFloor(generationStore, nodeId, generation);
167	      await bumpFloor(seqStore, nodeId, seq);
168	    },
169	  };
170	}
```

```
   * Re-resolves `ipnsName`'s CURRENT network `sequenceNumber` and compares it

   *
   * A null resolve (record not found -- e.g. a genuinely first publish) or a

   * reconcile against" and the check is skipped. A resolve() failure (network
   * error) is likewise treated as inconclusive rather than blocking the
   * mutation on a transient check -- the underlying CAS-guarded publish
   * remains the authoritative conflict detector.
   */
  private async reconcileFolderSequence(ipnsName: string, expectedSequence: bigint): Promise<void> {
    let resolved: { sequenceNumber: bigint } | null;
    try {
      resolved = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName, this.ctx);
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    if (resolved == null || typeof resolved.sequenceNumber !== 'bigint') return;

    // Durable ROT-07 anti-rollback gate (Gap 1 / SC#4): when a RotationHighWater
    // is injected, gate the freshly-resolved seq/generation through it BEFORE
    // the ReconcileStaleError equality check below. This call is deliberately
    // OUTSIDE the resolve try/catch above so a SequenceRegressionError /
    // GenerationRegressionError propagates to the mutation caller (and thence
    // to useMutationFailureUx's D-05 classifier) rather than being silenced.
    // Omitted when unconfigured -- zero enforcement, matching prior behavior.
    if (this.config.rotationHighWater) {

      // would silently truncate through Number() and corrupt the floor.
      if (
        resolved.sequenceNumber > BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) ||
        expectedSequence > BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)

        throw new Error(

        nodeId: ipnsName,
        seq: Number(resolved.sequenceNumber),
        generation: nodeGeneration,
        versionFloor: Number(expectedSequence),

    }

    if (resolved.sequenceNumber !== expectedSequence) {
      throw new ReconcileStaleError(ipnsName, expectedSequence, resolved.sequenceNumber);
    }
  }

   * and is a no-op. Failures on a single node do not abort the walk: an
   * unreadable node is recorded and NOT expanded further (its own children

   *

   */
  private async enumerateMoveDescendants(
    rootIpnsName: string,
    rootReadKey: Uint8Array,
    rootKind: PublishedNode['kind']
  ): Promise<{ readableIpnsNames: string[]; unreadableIpnsNames: string[] }> {
    const readableIpnsNames: string[] = [];
    const unreadableIpnsNames: string[] = [];

    if (rootKind !== 'folder' && rootKind !== 'root') {
      return { readableIpnsNames, unreadableIpnsNames };
    }

    const visited = new Set<string>([rootIpnsName]);
    const queue: Array<{ ipnsName: string; readKey: Uint8Array; isRoot: boolean }> = [
      { ipnsName: rootIpnsName, readKey: rootReadKey, isRoot: true },

    // Bound the walk so a pathological/corrupted tree can't hang the move.
    const MAX_NODES = 2000;
    let processed = 0;

    while (queue.length > 0 && processed < MAX_NODES) {
      const current = queue.shift();
      if (!current) break;
      processed++;

      try {
        let node: CoreNode;
        try {
          const record = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(current.ipnsName, this.ctx);
          if (!record) continue;
          const raw = await sdkCore.fetchFromIpfs(this.ctx, record.cid);

        } catch {

        for (const child of node.children ?? []) {
          if (visited.has(child.ipnsName)) continue;
          visited.add(child.ipnsName);

          try {
            const childRecord = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(child.ipnsName, this.ctx);
            if (!childRecord) throw new Error('child IPNS record not found');
            const rawChild = await sdkCore.fetchFromIpfs(this.ctx, childRecord.cid);
            const childPublished = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(rawChild)) as PublishedNode;
            const childReadKey = await unsealChildReadKey(
              child.readKeySealed,
              current.readKey,
              childPublished.id,
              childPublished.kind,
              child.generation
            );
            readableIpnsNames.push(child.ipnsName);
            queue.push({ ipnsName: child.ipnsName, readKey: childReadKey, isRoot: false });
          } catch {
            unreadableIpnsNames.push(child.ipnsName);
          }
        }
      } finally {
        // The children loop unseals with this key, so it may only be zeroed
        // after the loop (this queue entry is the key's terminal owner).
        if (!current.isRoot) current.readKey.fill(0);
      }
    }

    return { readableIpnsNames, unreadableIpnsNames };
  }
```

```
   * Only the sharer (owner) can update it — the owner drives grant re-mint on
   * key rotation (D-10/D-11, 68-07 owner reconcile). The server never
   * re-encrypts — it persists the client-supplied ciphertext as-is.
   */
  async updateGrant(
    shareId: string,
    sharerId: string,
    readDescriptorRef: string,
    rootGeneration: string
  ): Promise<void> {
    // Row-locked transaction: the anti-rollback check below must see the row
    // a concurrent PATCH just committed, not a stale pre-race read (TOCTOU).
    await this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {
      const share = await manager.findOne(Share, {
        where: { id: shareId },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });

      if (!share) {
        throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
      }

      if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
        throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update the grant');
      }

      // Anti-rollback: the grant's rootGeneration seeds the recipient's durable
      // generation floor on first contact, so a replayed/duplicate PATCH must
      // never regress it below the stored value.
      if (BigInt(rootGeneration) < BigInt(share.rootGeneration)) {
        throw new ConflictException(
          'rootGeneration regression rejected: grants only advance the generation'
        );
      }

      share.readDescriptorRef = Buffer.from(readDescriptorRef, 'hex');
      share.rootGeneration = rootGeneration;
      await manager.save(share);
    });
  }
}
```

> `890-967`: _🗄️ Data Integrity & Integration_ | _🟠 Major_ | _🏗️ Heavy lift_

> **Dirty-resume republish result is silently dropped, leaving caller's folderTree stale.**

> On the dirty-resume path (`rootResult.skipped === true`, `isDirty === true`), when `frontier.length > 0` the code seeds `parentTracking` for the root and, once all frontier children finish, republishes the root via `updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` (line ~1042/1110) — this bumps the root's on-chain IPNS `sequenceNumber`. However, the final return (line 1192) unconditionally returns `undefined` whenever `rootResult.skipped` is true, regardless of whether this dirty-resume walk actually republished the root with a new sequence number.
> 
> Since `performScopeExitRotation` (packages/sdk/src/client.ts) only refreshes its in-memory `folderTree` entry when `rotationResult` is truthy, this specific case leaves the client's cached `sequenceNumber` stale after a real publish occurred — a subsequent same-session mutation on this folder will hit `reconcileFolderSequence`'s `ReconcileStaleError` and defer permanently until a full reload. This is exactly the class of bug "ROT-07 Gap 2" fixed for the normal path, but the dirty-resume-with-pending-children case isn't covered.
> 
> <details>
> <summary>🛠️ Sketch: surface the republished sequence on the dirty-resume path too</summary>
> 
> ```diff
>    if (rootResult.skipped) {
>      // Resume path: root already committed in a prior run.
>      const { isDirty, frontier } = await verifySubtreeClean(rootNodeIpnsName, rootReadKey, ctx);
>      if (!isDirty) {
>        jobRecord.status = 'complete';
>        if (jobRecord.persistCallback) await jobRecord.persistCallback(jobRecord);
>        return undefined;
>      }
>      ...
> +    // Track whether this dirty-resume walk republishes the root so the final
> +    // return can report the up-to-date sequenceNumber even without a fresh key.
>    }
>    ...
> -  if (rootResult.skipped) {
> -    return undefined;
> -  }
> +  if (rootResult.skipped) {
> +    return dirtyResumeRootRepublish
> +      ? { readKey: rootReadKey, generation: rootNode.generation, sequenceNumber: dirtyResumeRootRepublish.newSequenceNumber }
> +      : undefined;
> +  }
> ```
> </details>
> 
> 
> 
> 
> 
> Also applies to: 1180-1199

```
  // Re-wrapping needs the full set — paginate through all pages
  const allShares = await fetchAllSentShares();

/**
 * Fetch ALL sent shares by paginating through the API.
 * The API enforces a max limit of 100 per page.
 */
async function fetchAllSentShares(): Promise<SentShare[]> {
  const pageSize = 100;
  let offset = 0;
  const allShares: SentShare[] = [];

  while (true) {
    const { shares, total } = await fetchSentShares(pageSize, offset);
    allShares.push(...shares);
    offset += shares.length;
    if (offset >= total || shares.length === 0) break;
  }

  // Lazy backfill (decision A2): re-wrap and persist itemNameEncrypted for any
  // legacy plaintext rows. Best-effort and idempotent (shouldBackfill skips rows
  // already carrying ciphertext), fire-and-forget so the list load never blocks.
  void backfillSentShareItemNames(allShares).catch((err) =>
    logger.warn('[share] itemName backfill pass failed', err)
  );

  return allShares;
}

/**
 * Check if a folder (by IPNS name) has any active shares.
 * Used to decide whether post-upload re-wrapping is needed.
 */
export async function hasActiveShares(folderIpnsName: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const shares = await ensureFreshSentShares();
  return shares.some((s) => s.ipnsName === folderIpnsName);
```

<details>
<summary>apps/web/src/services/rotation-driver.service.ts (1)</summary><blockquote>

`59-101`: _🚀 Performance & Scalability_ | _🔵 Trivial_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**IndexedDB connections opened per call, never closed.**

Every `putJobCheckpoint`/`deleteJobCheckpoint`/`getAllJobCheckpoints` call opens a fresh `openJobDB()` connection that is never closed, accumulating open handles over a session and risking a blocked upgrade if `JOB_DB_VERSION` is ever bumped while old connections remain open. Consider caching a single connection promise at module scope and reusing it.

```
IndexedDB helpers are opening a new connection on every call and never closing
it, which can leave stale handles around. Update the openJobDB,
putJobCheckpoint, deleteJobCheckpoint, and getAllJobCheckpoints flow to reuse a
```

<details>
<summary>apps/web/src/services/rotation-state.service.ts (1)</summary><blockquote>

`42-111`: _🚀 Performance & Scalability_ | _🔵 Trivial_ | _⚡ Quick win_

**Cache the IndexedDB connection instead of reopening per get/put.**

`idbGet`/`idbPut` each call `openRotationDB()` independently and never close the resulting connection. Since this backs `enforceResolved`, called on every rotation-participating `resolveIpnsRecord` (potentially every navigation), each resolve can open 1-2 fresh `IDBDatabase` connections that are never released — real overhead on a hot path, and a future `DB_VERSION` bump would risk `onblocked` since old connections stay open indefinitely. Cache a single connection-open promise at module scope and reuse it across `get`/`put` calls.



<details>
<summary>🔧 Proposed fix: cache the open connection</summary>

```diff
-function openRotationDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
-  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
-    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
-    request.onupgradeneeded = () => { ... };
-    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
-    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
-  });
-}
+let dbPromise: Promise<IDBDatabase> | null = null;
+function openRotationDB(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
+  if (!dbPromise) {
+    dbPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);
+      request.onupgradeneeded = () => { ... };
+      request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);
+      request.onerror = () => {
+        dbPromise = null;
+        reject(request.error);
+      };
+    });
+  }
+  return dbPromise;
+}
```
</details>

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/state/rotation-high-water.ts",
  "old_string": "async function bumpFloor(\n  store: HighWaterStore,\n  nodeId: string,\n  candidate: number\n): Promise<number> {\n  const current = await readFloor(store, nodeId);\n  if (current === undefined || candidate > current) {\n    await store.put(nodeId, candidate);\n    return candidate;\n  }\n  return current;\n}",
  "new_string": "async function bumpFloor(\n  store: HighWaterStore,\n  nodeId: string,\n  candidate: number\n): Promise<number> {\n  const current = await readFloor(store, nodeId);\n  // V5 applies to writes too: never persist a malformed candidate (NaN,\n  // negative, fractional) -- the floor stays where it is.\n  if (!isValidFloorValue(candidate)) {\n    return current ?? 0;\n  }\n  if (current === undefined || candidate > current) {\n    await store.put(nodeId, candidate);\n    return candidate;\n  }\n  return current;\n}"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "    async enforceResolved({ nodeId, seq, generation, versionFloor }) {\n      const generationFloor = await readFloor(generationStore, nodeId);\n      if (generationFloor !== undefined && generation < generationFloor) {\n        throw new GenerationRegressionError(nodeId, generationFloor, generation);\n      }\n\n      const seqFloor = await readFloor(seqStore, nodeId);\n      if (seqFloor === undefined) {\n        // Cold-device / first-contact: apply the owner-vouched versionFloor gate.\n        if (seq < versionFloor) {\n          throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, versionFloor, seq);\n        }\n      } else if (seq < seqFloor) {\n        throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, seqFloor, seq);\n      }",
  "new_string": "    async enforceResolved({ nodeId, seq, generation, versionFloor }) {\n      // Fail-closed on the LIVE inputs too, not just stored values: NaN\n      // compares false against everything in JS, so a malformed generation/\n      // seq would otherwise sail past both `<` regression checks below.\n      // floor=-1 marks \"input itself invalid\" (no valid floor was consulted).\n      if (!isValidFloorValue(generation)) {\n        throw new GenerationRegressionError(nodeId, -1, generation);\n      }\n      if (!isValidFloorValue(seq)) {\n        throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, -1, seq);\n      }\n\n      const generationFloor = await readFloor(generationStore, nodeId);\n      if (generationFloor !== undefined && generation < generationFloor) {\n        throw new GenerationRegressionError(nodeId, generationFloor, generation);\n      }\n\n      const seqFloor = await readFloor(seqStore, nodeId);\n      if (seqFloor === undefined) {\n        // Cold-device / first-contact: apply the owner-vouched versionFloor\n        // gate. A malformed versionFloor is rejected rather than treated as\n        // \"no gate\" -- first contact is exactly when the gate matters most.\n        if (!isValidFloorValue(versionFloor) || seq < versionFloor) {\n          throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, versionFloor, seq);\n        }\n      } else if (seq < seqFloor) {\n        throw new SequenceRegressionError(nodeId, seqFloor, seq);\n      }"
}
```

```json
{
```

```json
  "old_string": "/** D-06: dispatches the terminal defer-exhausted notice with a manual Retry action. */\nfunction dispatchDeferExhausted<T>(mutationFn: () => Promise<T>): void {\n  const id = useNotificationStore\n    .getState()\n    .addNotification('error', \"Couldn't complete securely — retry.\", {\n      label: 'Retry',\n      onClick: () => {\n        // Replace, don't stack: a persistent failure re-dispatches a fresh\n        // exhaustion toast, so drop this one before re-running.\n        useNotificationStore.getState().dismissNotification(id);\n        void retryAfterExhaustion(mutationFn);\n      },\n    });\n}\n\nasync function retryAfterExhaustion<T>(mutationFn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await runWithFailureUx(mutationFn);\n  } catch {\n    // The retried run dispatches its own notice for any repeat failure.\n  }\n}",
  "new_string": "/** D-06: dispatches the terminal defer-exhausted notice with a manual Retry action. */\nfunction dispatchDeferExhausted<T>(\n  mutationFn: () => Promise<T>,\n  opts: RunWithFailureUxOptions\n): void {\n  const id = useNotificationStore\n    .getState()\n    .addNotification('error', \"Couldn't complete securely — retry.\", {\n      label: 'Retry',\n      onClick: () => {\n        // Replace, don't stack: a persistent failure re-dispatches a fresh\n        // exhaustion toast, so drop this one before re-running.\n        useNotificationStore.getState().dismissNotification(id);\n        void retryAfterExhaustion(mutationFn, opts);\n      },\n    });\n}\n\nasync function retryAfterExhaustion<T>(\n  mutationFn: () => Promise<T>,\n  opts: RunWithFailureUxOptions\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    // Thread the ORIGINAL opts through the manual retry so a subsequent\n    // stale-write failure still offers the refreshWriteAccess path.\n    await runWithFailureUx(mutationFn, opts);\n  } catch {\n    // The retried run dispatches its own notice for any repeat failure.\n  }\n}"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "async function decodeSentGrants(): Promise<DecodedSentGrant[]> {\n  const { shares } = await sharesControllerGetSentShares();\n  const decoded: DecodedSentGrant[] = [];\n  for (const share of shares) {\n    try {\n      // Wire format is bare hex, but normalize a 0x prefix defensively --\n      // hexToBytes would otherwise choke and one bad row must not abort the\n      // whole reconcile sweep.\n      const bareHex = share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')\n        ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)\n        : share.recipientPublicKey;\n      decoded.push({\n        shareId: share.shareId,\n        recipientPublicKey: hexToBytes(bareHex),\n        isRevoked: false,\n        rootNodeId: share.rootNodeId,\n        rootIpnsName: share.rootIpnsName,\n      });\n    } catch (error) {\n      logger.error(\n        `[owner-reconcile] Skipping sent grant ${share.shareId}: recipientPublicKey decode failed:`,\n        safeError(error)\n      );\n    }\n  }\n  return decoded;\n}",
  "new_string": "async function decodeSentGrants(): Promise<DecodedSentGrant[]> {\n  const decoded: DecodedSentGrant[] = [];\n  // Paginate through ALL sent shares (the API caps limit at 100 per page) --\n  // a first-page-only read would leave grants beyond page 1 unreconciled.\n  const pageSize = 100;\n  let offset = 0;\n  for (;;) {\n    const { shares, total } = await sharesControllerGetSentShares({ limit: pageSize, offset });\n    for (const share of shares) {\n      try {\n        // Wire format is bare hex, but normalize a 0x prefix defensively --\n        // hexToBytes would otherwise choke and one bad row must not abort the\n        // whole reconcile sweep.\n        const bareHex = share.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')\n          ? share.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)\n          : share.recipientPublicKey;\n        decoded.push({\n          shareId: share.shareId,\n          recipientPublicKey: hexToBytes(bareHex),\n          isRevoked: false,\n          rootNodeId: share.rootNodeId,\n          rootIpnsName: share.rootIpnsName,\n        });\n      } catch (error) {\n        logger.error(\n          `[owner-reconcile] Skipping sent grant ${share.shareId}: recipientPublicKey decode failed:`,\n          safeError(error)\n        );\n      }\n    }\n    offset += shares.length;\n    if (offset >= total || shares.length === 0) break;\n  }\n  return decoded;\n}"
}
```

```
1	---
2	created: 2026-07-02
3	title: Rotation hardening follow-ups deferred from Phase 68 PR review
4	area: sdk/web
5	files:
6	  - packages/sdk/src/state/rotation-high-water.ts
7	  - packages/sdk/src/client.ts
8	  - packages/sdk-core/src/rotation/engine.ts
9	  - apps/web/src/services/rotation-driver.service.ts
10	---
11	
12	## Problem
13	
14	Four CodeRabbit findings from the Phase 68 ship review were real but too architectural/risky for a ship-time hot patch:
15	
16	1. **Cross-store bump atomicity** (`rotation-high-water.ts#enforceResolved`): `bumpFloor(generationStore)` then `bumpFloor(seqStore)` run sequentially; if the second write fails, the two floors diverge until the next successful resolve. Both floors only ever rise, so the divergence is under-protective (not rollback-accepting), but a clean fix needs an atomic multi-store transaction API through the `HighWaterStore` seam — which also has to survive the D-08 in-memory degradation path.
17	2. **`RotateReadResult.readKey` terminal ownership** (`client.ts#performScopeExitRotation` / `engine.ts#rotateReadFromNode`): the returned `readKey` aliases the engine's `rootResult.childReadKey` frontier buffer. `performScopeExitRotation` already takes a defensive copy into `folderTree`; the ORIGINAL buffer is currently never zeroed. Zeroing it in the client contradicts the phase's documented T-68-12-02 decision and risks the exact callee-zeroes-shared-buffer class that broke 48/89 sdk-e2e once before. Decide the terminal owner deliberately (probably: engine hands over ownership on return → client zeroes after its copy), update the D-09 doc comments on BOTH sides, and prove with sdk-e2e.
18	3. **Per-call IndexedDB connections** (`rotation-driver.service.ts`): `openJobDB` opens a fresh connection per checkpoint call and never closes it. Cache a shared open-promise (with invalidation on `onversionchange`/close-on-logout).
19	4. **Single-root badge tracking** (`rotation-driver.service.ts#persistJob`): `activeRootNodeId` is module-global, so concurrent rotations on different roots misclassify each other's root-cut/tail-walk phases and a finishing root resets the badge while another is mid-walk. Durable checkpoints are unaffected (delete is keyed by the finished job's own rootNodeId) — badge-UX only. Track per-root (Set keyed by rootNodeId) and only reset the badge when the set drains.
20	
21	## Solution
22	
23	One small hardening plan touching the four sites above; items 1–2 need sdk-e2e re-runs, items 3–4 are web-only. Related: the existing rotation-fresh-record-resume todo also touches the driver.
24
```

```json
  "new_string": "4. **Single-root badge tracking** (`rotation-driver.service.ts#persistJob`): `activeRootNodeId` is module-global, so concurrent rotations on different roots misclassify each other's root-cut/tail-walk phases and a finishing root resets the badge while another is mid-walk. Durable checkpoints are unaffected (delete is keyed by the finished job's own rootNodeId) — badge-UX only. Track per-root (Set keyed by rootNodeId) and only reset the badge when the set drains.\n\nTwo more from CodeRabbit's second-pass PR review (2026-07-02, PR `#587` re-review of the fix commit):\n\n5. **Reconcile gate uses a cached generation, not the freshly-resolved one** (`client.ts#reconcileFolderSequence`): `resolveIpnsRecord` only returns `cid`/`sequenceNumber`/`signatureVerified` — the generation lives inside the sealed node payload, so `enforceResolved` is fed `folderTree.get(ipnsName)?.nodeGeneration ?? 0` (cached local state). Safe direction-wise (floors are monotonic-max, a cached value can only under-bump), but the gate cannot detect a generation regression in the record it just resolved. Either thread the resolved generation through (requires fetching + unsealing inside the reconcile path) or make the cached-fallback contract explicit on `resolveIpnsRecord`/`reconcileFolderSequence`.\n6. **Dirty-resume republish result silently dropped** (`engine.ts#rotateReadFromNode` ~890–1199): on the resume path (`rootResult.skipped === true`) with a dirty subtree, the walk can republish the root (bumping the IPNS seq) yet the function still returns `undefined`, so `performScopeExitRotation` never refreshes `folderTree.sequenceNumber` — the exact stale-seq → permanent `ReconcileStaleError` class ROT-07 Gap 2 fixed for the normal path. Surface the republished sequence from the dirty-resume walk and return a truthy result when a real publish occurred.\n\n## Solution\n\nOne small hardening plan touching the sites above; items 1–2 and 5–6 need sdk-e2e re-runs, items 3–4 are web-only. Related: the existing rotation-fresh-record-resume todo also touches the driver."
}
```

```
  if (!resolved || !rotation) {
    return resolved;
  }

  // Fail closed BEFORE any floor mutation: an absent-signature record
  // (signatureVerified=false, tolerated by D-03 for legacy names) must never
  // seed or bump a durable floor — a relay could otherwise forge a huge seq
  // and permanently DoS the node. Rotation-participating nodes are always
  // published signed, so an unverified record here is itself a red flag.
  if (!resolved.signatureVerified) {
    throw new Error(
      `IPNS resolve for rotation-participating node ${ipnsName} returned an unverified record — refusing to gate floors on it`
    );
  }
  // The durable floor stores JS numbers; a sequence beyond MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  // would silently truncate through Number() and corrupt the floor.
  if (resolved.sequenceNumber > BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)) {
    throw new Error(
      `IPNS sequence number for ${ipnsName} exceeds Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER — refusing lossy floor conversion`
    );
  }

  // Durable floors are keyed by the resolved name itself (no separate node id
  // in the read plane — see SealedChildRef), so the caller cannot key a floor
  // under the wrong node.
  if (rotation.rootGeneration !== undefined) {
    await seedFromGrant(ipnsName, rotation.rootGeneration);
  }

  await enforceResolved({
    nodeId: ipnsName,
    seq: Number(resolved.sequenceNumber),
    generation: rotation.generation,
    versionFloor: rotation.versionFloor,
  });

  return resolved;
}
```

> AGENT

All 7 new review findings are triaged and closed out. Summary of this round:

**Fixed in `11caee2a3`** (pushed, all local gates green — SDK suite 265 passed incl. 5 new tests, root typecheck exit 0, eslint clean):
- `enforceResolved` now rejects NaN/negative/fractional live `generation`/`seq` fail-closed (NaN comparisons were silently bypassing the regression gate), a malformed cold-device `versionFloor` rejects instead of disabling the first-contact gate, and `bumpFloor` never persists a malformed candidate
- `useMutationFailureUx` threads the original `opts` through the defer-exhaustion Retry, so the `refreshWriteAccess` path survives a manual retry
- `decodeSentGrants` paginates the sent-grants fetch (was first-page-only — owners with >50 grants would silently miss reconciles)
- `enumerateMoveDescendants` zeroes readKeys left queued when the `MAX_NODES` cutoff exits the walk early
- The rotation-durability e2e spec opens IndexedDB without a pinned version

**Deferred to the rotation-hardening todo** (items 5–6 appended): reconcile-gate generation sourced from cache rather than the freshly-resolved record (needs fetch+unseal in the reconcile path), and the engine's dirty-resume republish result being dropped (same stale-seq class as ROT-07 Gap 2, too deep for a ship-time patch).

**Skipped with reasoning**: Greptile's `BigInt(null)` P1 — the column is `bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` since its table was created, and defaulting corrupt data to `0n` would *weaken* the anti-rollback check. Greptile replied accepting the reasoning.

All 11 threads are resolved (0 unresolved, no duplicate replies — the extra comments are Greptile's acknowledgment and CodeRabbit transient bot errors). CI is now re-running on `11caee2a3`; the background poll will report when checks settle, after which I'll do a final late-thread sweep and deliver the closing report.

```
{"body":"\u003ca href=\"#\"\u003e\u003cimg alt=\"P1\" src=\"https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p1.svg?v=9\" align=\"top\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e \u003ca href=\"#\"\u003e\u003cimg alt=\"security\" src=\"https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/Security.svg?v=2\" align=\"top\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e **`enforceResolved` called on unverified IPNS record in write path**\n\n`sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord` can return a record with `signatureVerified: false` (confirmed by `apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts`, which calls the same function and explicitly checks `!resolved.signatureVerified` before calling `enforceResolved`). Here, the type annotation is narrowed to `{ sequenceNumber: bigint } | null`, hiding that field, and no signature guard is applied before `rotationHighWater.enforceResolved`.\n\nA relay that returns an unverified record with an artificially large `seq` would cause `bumpFloor` to store that inflated value in the shared IDB seq floor. On the next genuine mutation, `resolveIpnsRecord` returns the real (lower-seq) record, causing `enforceResolved` to throw `SequenceRegressionError` — which `useMutationFailureUx` classifies as terminal (no retry). A single relay intercept permanently blocks all further mutations on that folder until the IDB store is manually cleared.\n\nThe read path (`ipns.service.ts`) explicitly documents and enforces this guard; the write path should be symmetric. Fix by widening the type annotation to include `signatureVerified: boolean` and early-returning (or throwing) when the field is false, matching the existing pattern in `ipns.service.ts`.","line":548,"path":"packages/sdk/src/client.ts"}
```

```json
  "new_string": "  private async reconcileFolderSequence(ipnsName: string, expectedSequence: bigint): Promise<void> {\n    let resolved: { sequenceNumber: bigint; signatureVerified: boolean } | null;\n    try {\n      resolved = await sdkCore.resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName, this.ctx);\n    } catch {\n      return;\n    }\n    if (resolved == null || typeof resolved.sequenceNumber !== 'bigint') return;\n\n    // Durable ROT-07 anti-rollback gate (Gap 1 / SC#4): when a RotationHighWater\n    // is injected, gate the freshly-resolved seq/generation through it BEFORE\n    // the ReconcileStaleError equality check below. The late Greptile P1 is handled: `reconcileFolderSequence` was calling `enforceResolved` on a resolve without the `signatureVerified` guard the read path has — a forged relay record with an inflated seq could have bumped the durable floor and permanently wedged mutations. Fixed in `a781637f8` (guard + regression test, 266 SDK tests green, typecheck/lint clean), pushed, thread replied and resolved (12/12 threads now resolved). CI is re-running on the new commit; the background poll will report when it settles, then I'll do one more late-thread sweep and close out.
```

```
{"body":"\u003ca href=\"#\"\u003e\u003cimg alt=\"P1\" src=\"https://greptile-static-assets.s3.amazonaws.com/badges/p1.svg?v=9\" align=\"top\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e **\"Refresh access\" toast not dismissed when clicked**\n\n`dispatchWriteDescriptorStale` discards the `addNotification` return value (the notification id), so the \"Refresh access\" toast can never be dismissed by its own action handler. After the user clicks the button, `retryAfterRefresh` runs and dispatches a follow-up toast (\"Write access could not be refreshed.\" or, if the re-run fails, \"Write access revoked.\" via `_afterRefresh: true`), leaving the original \"Refresh access\" toast stacked on top.\n\nCompare with `dispatchDeferExhausted` at line 86, which was explicitly fixed for this exact pattern: `const id = addNotification(...)` and `dismissNotification(id)` before re-running. The `dispatchWriteDescriptorStale` path has the same structure but the fix was not applied. A user who hits a persistent `CannotWriteUntilRefetchError` will accumulate one stale \"Refresh access\" toast per attempt.\n\n```suggestion\n  if (opts.refreshWriteAccess \u0026\u0026 !opts._afterRefresh) {\n    const id = useNotificationStore\n      .getState()\n      .addNotification('error', 'Write failed — access may be out of date.', {\n        label: 'Refresh access',\n        onClick: () =\u003e {\n          useNotificationStore.getState().dismissNotification(id);\n          void retryAfterRefresh(mutationFn, opts);\n        },\n      });\n  } else {\n```","line":177,"path":"apps/web/src/hooks/useMutationFailureUx.ts"}
```

```json
{
  "command": "cd /Users/myankelev/.claude/jobs/d2e29299/tmp && cat > resolve-greptile2.py << 'EOF'\nimport subprocess, time, sys\n\nTID = \"PRRT_kwDOQ6DXvM6N4ZMY\"\nBODY = \"Fixed in c3c215d5b: `dispatchWriteDescriptorStale` now captures the notification id and dismisses the refresh-access toast in its own onClick before re-running, matching the anti-stacking pattern used by the defer-exhausted Retry.\"\n\ndef gql(query, **vars):\n    cmd = [\"gh\", \"api\", \"graphql\", \"-f\", f\"query={query}\"]\n    for k, v in vars.items():\n        cmd += [\"-f\", f\"{k}={v}\"]\n    return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)\n\nREPLY_M = 'mutation($tid: ID!, $body: String!) { addPullRequestReviewThreadReply(input: {pullRequestReviewThreadId: $tid, body: $body}) { comment { id } } }'\nRESOLVE_M = 'mutation($tid: ID!) { resolveReviewThread(input: {threadId: $tid}) { thread { isResolved } } }'\n\nfor attempt in range(3):\n    r = gql(REPLY_M, tid=TID, body=BODY)\n    if r.returncode == 0: break\n    print(f\"reply retry: {r.stderr[:200]}\", file=sys.stderr); time.sleep(3)\nelse:\n    sys.exit(\"REPLY FAILED\")\nfor attempt in range(3):\n    r = gql(RESOLVE_M, tid=TID)\n    if r.returncode == 0:\n        print(\"OK resolved\"); break\n    print(f\"resolve retry: {r.stderr[:200]}\", file=sys.stderr); time.sleep(3)\nelse:\n    sys.exit(\"RESOLVE FAILED\")\nEOF\nenv -u GITHUB_TOKEN python3 resolve-greptile2.py",
  "description": "Reply to and resolve the toast-stacking thread",
  "timeout": 120000,
  "dangerouslyDisableSandbox": true
}
```

```json
{
  "command": "for i in $(seq 1 30); do\n  OUT=$(env -u GITHUB_TOKEN gh pr checks 587 2>/dev/null)\n  if [ -z \"$OUT\" ]; then echo \"poll $i: gh transient error, retrying\"; sleep 60; continue; fi\n  PEND=$(echo \"$OUT\" | awk -F'\\t' '$2==\"pending\"' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n  FAIL=$(echo \"$OUT\" | awk -F'\\t' '$2==\"fail\"' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')\n  echo \"poll $i: pending=$PEND fail=$FAIL\"\n  if [ \"$PEND\" = \"0\" ]; then\n    echo \"ALL SETTLED fail=$FAIL\"\n    echo \"$OUT\" | awk -F'\\t' '$2!=\"pass\"'\n    exit 0\n  fi\n  sleep 60\ndone\necho \"TIMED OUT\"",
  "description": "Poll checks on the toast-fix commit until settled",
```
